**[00:13:03.940] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000048)

**[00:13:09.080] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000049)

**[00:13:16.760] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000051)

**[00:13:24.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000052)

**[00:13:26.060] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000053)  
I was looking at the fiscal year 2027,

**[00:13:29.960] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000054)  
DMW and OWA have a combined proposed expenditure program of 11.615 billion pesos,

**[00:13:38.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000055)

**[00:13:45.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000056)

**[00:13:48.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000057)

**[00:13:54.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000058)  
and 4.349 billion will go to OWA.

**[00:13:59.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000059)  
Sa budget po para sa ating migrant workers,

**[00:14:03.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000060)

**[00:14:07.980] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000061)  
Talagang ang tunay na sukatan ay yung pong kung may OFW bang naprotektahan,

**[00:14:14.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000062)

**[00:14:15.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000063)  
meron po ba tayong nabigyan ng hustisya,

**[00:14:18.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000064)

**[00:14:20.940] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000065)

**[00:14:24.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000066)

**[00:14:28.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000067)

**[00:14:30.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000068)

**[00:14:34.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000069)

**[00:14:41.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000070)

**[00:14:45.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000071)

**[00:14:53.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000072)

**[00:14:54.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000073)

**[00:14:58.080] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000074)

**[00:15:00.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000075)  
Central to this response is the Action Fund. And the reports that this representation had received from July 2023 to July 2026, the fund assisted more than 606,000 OFWs.

**[00:15:17.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000076)  
And from January to July of this year alone, it provided 2.95 billion pesos in assistance to 145,448 beneficiaries. And in response to the Middle East crisis, its 2026 funding was increased to 5 billion pesos.

**[00:15:39.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000077)  
Sa likod po ng bawat numerong ito ay muli, isang OFW na nangangailangan ng pagpapagamot, ticket na pauwi.

**[00:15:47.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000078)  
Agarang saklolo. And for 2027, the Action Fund must remain adequately funded and ready for both the daily needs of distressed workers and a possible large-scale crisis.

**[00:16:01.940] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000079)  
Now, looking at OWWA's Emergency Repatriation Fund, the proposed funding is at 977.8 million, which is 24% lower than...

**[00:16:17.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000080)

**[00:16:31.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000081)

**[00:16:47.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000082)

**[00:16:53.740] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000083)

**[00:17:07.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000084)

Nasusukat din

**[00:17:17.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000086)

**[00:23:56.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000169)  
our budget sponsor and the good Secretary,

**[00:23:59.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000170)  
the DMW's National Expenditure Program for Fiscal Year 2027 is 11.616 billion.

**[00:24:07.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000171)

**[00:24:11.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000172)

**[00:24:25.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000174)

**[00:24:33.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000175)

**[00:24:37.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000176)

**[00:24:40.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000177)  
For the Office of the Secretary,

**[00:24:43.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000178)  
budget decreased by 193 million while OWA increased by 64 million.

**[00:24:50.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000179)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:24:52.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000180)  
By expense class, our personnel services is at 4.026 billion or 34% share,

**[00:25:01.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000181)  
MOOE at 7.513 billion and capital outlay at 77 million.

**[00:25:10.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000182)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:25:12.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000183)  
By cost structure, our general administrative support is 1.837 billion

**[00:25:21.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000184)  
and the operations is at 8.9 billion.

**[00:25:27.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000185)  
For OSEC, the budget under the GAS decreased by 88 million or 9%

**[00:25:34.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000186)  
and operations also decreased by 105 million

**[00:25:38.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000187)  
compared against the 2026 GAA,

**[00:25:40.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000188)  
which can be attributed to the significant decrease of the OFW hospital budget,

**[00:25:45.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000189)  
MWO budget and the national maritime budget.

**[00:25:49.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000190)  
For OWA, budget for GAS decreased by 116 million or 11%

**[00:25:55.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000191)  
while the operations increased by 179 million

**[00:25:59.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000192)  
for Alagang OWA and workers auxiliary support projects.

**[00:26:03.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000193)  
Next slide.

**[00:26:05.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000194)  
In terms of programs for OSEC,

**[00:26:07.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000195)  
there's a slight budget increases in the overseas employment and welfare program,

**[00:26:13.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000196)  
regulatory program, labor migration policy and international cooperation.

**[00:26:18.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000197)  
Meanwhile, the general administration services and NMPs budgets decreased as mentioned

**[00:26:24.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000198)

**[00:26:27.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000199)

**[00:26:33.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000200)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:26:34.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000201)  
For OWA,

**[00:26:35.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000202)  
GAS also decreased by 12 million.

**[00:26:39.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000203)  
Meanwhile, the welfare services increased particularly the Alagang OWA and its auxiliary program.

**[00:26:46.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000204)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:26:48.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000205)  
For the implementing office,

**[00:26:49.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000206)

**[00:26:55.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000207)

**[00:27:00.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000208)

**[00:27:10.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000211)

**[00:27:12.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000212)  
OFW hospital is at 6%

**[00:27:15.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000213)  
and NMPs budget share is 3%.

**[00:27:18.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000214)

**[00:27:40.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000220)

**[00:27:44.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000221)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:27:48.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000222)  
On the salient features of our 2027 NEP for OSEC,

**[00:27:53.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000223)  
and that's 7.267 billion,

**[00:28:02.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000226)

**[00:28:54.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000241)

For OWA, the 4.349 billion under the 2027 NEP,

The

**[00:30:17.900] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000268)

**[00:30:19.900] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000269)

**[00:39:31.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000510)  
dear Honorable Senator,

**[00:39:33.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000511)  
Senator Erwin Tulfo,

**[00:39:35.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000512)  
Senator Joel Villanueva,

**[00:39:37.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000513)

**[00:39:39.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000514)  
Maikling ano lang pong dagdag sa naging presentasyon po ng aming mother department, the Department of Migrant Workers, [unclear] po sa ating chairperson ang status po ngayon ng Emergency Repatriation Fund.

**[00:39:56.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000515)

**[00:40:05.360] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000516)  
Lalong-lalo na po, as we speak right now, nasa crisis pa rin po. In crisis mode pa rin po tayo doon po sa regyon kung saan napakarami po nating OFWs.

**[00:40:17.920] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000517)  
Layon po ng Department of Migrant Workers at ng OWA na maging laging handa, protektahan ang ating mga OFWs.

**[00:40:26.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000518)  
Ang aming pong NEP as mentioned ay 4.349 billion or may slight na increase ng 1.48%.

**[00:40:36.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000519)  
Next slide please.

**[00:40:37.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000520)  
Ngunit itong 1.48% ay na-mention din po ito, nasabi ni ating mahal na chairperson, na nabawasan po ang Emergency Repatriation Fund ng 308.778 million.

**[00:40:55.980] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000521)  
Kasama rin po.

**[00:40:56.780] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000522)  
Kasama rin po dyan ay nabawasan din ang capital outlay ng 72 million at ang ating MOOE gas po ng 74.9 million.

**[00:41:07.900] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000523)  
Next slide please.

**[00:41:10.900] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000524)

**[00:41:14.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000525)  
Dahil ang aming pong hiling na suporta ay in the amount po of 2 billion pesos.

**[00:41:26.780] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000526)  
Ito pong dalawang bilyong ito ay magiging isang napaka-importante pong aspeto, yung dagdag na resources para lagi pong maging handa, protektahan ang ating mga OFWs.

**[00:41:40.660] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000527)  
Ang Emergency Repatriation Fund will allow us na maging handa bago ang krisis.

**[00:41:46.660] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000528)  
Ang hinihingi po namin karagdagang 575 million.

**[00:41:50.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000529)  
Ang institusyon na siyang ang OWA ay hinihiling din po namin maging handa.

**[00:41:56.780] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000530)

**[00:42:03.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000531)  
Mamaya po ay hihighlight ko po ito, 770 million.

**[00:42:08.460] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000532)  
Gusto rin po namin maging handa saan man sila naroon under the Alagang OWA program at 595 million at WASP.

**[00:42:18.040] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000533)  
Ito po kasama po rito mga OFW lounges, global center, pati na rin po ang mga local hires abroad.

**[00:42:25.980] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000534)

**[00:42:26.700] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000535)  
At ang gusto po namin, padagdagang pang mga medical retainers.

**[00:42:31.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000536)  
Ang pangapat po ay maging handa para sa kinabukasan.

**[00:42:34.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000537)  
Para sa mga anak po ng mga OFWs.

**[00:42:37.620] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000538)  
Yung pong nabuong Congressional Migrant Workers Scholarship Fund under the Migrant Workers Protection Act 31 years ago

**[00:42:47.560] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000539)  
ay hindi po nabigyan ng dagdag na pondo at yun lang pong unang bigay noong ginawa po ang batas na iyon.

**[00:43:31.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000548)

**[00:43:42.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000549)

**[00:43:51.280] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000550)  
Ito po ay ang tumutulong para iuwi ang ating mga kababayang.

**[00:43:55.620] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000551)  
Ang sumasagot po nito kasama ng Action Fund on the part of OWA ay ang Emergency Repatriation Fund.

umuwi pa yung ating OFW. Pag-uwi po niya, pipila pa po siya sa ibang opisina. Nawawala yung araw kasama yung kanyang pamilya.

**[00:51:34.460] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000755)  
So kasama po natin dito, Mr. Chair, even po ang LTO, nagre-renew na rin ho ng driver's license abroad.

**[00:51:43.560] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000756)  
Marami po tayong mga kababayan na drivers na ang trabaho na kailangan po nito.

**[00:51:49.100] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000757)  
Aside from, of course, the services ng iba't iba pa pong ahensyang kasama namin.

**[00:51:55.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000758)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:51:59.460] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000759)  
Sa 5.59 million active at inactive members po ng OWA, currently, we have 109 service points o mga pasilidad

**[00:52:09.880] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000760)  
kung saan pwedeng lumapit ang ating mga kababayan, including our seafarers, para sa serbisyo.

**[00:52:15.900] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000761)  
So layon po namin, under the alagang OWA,

**[00:52:18.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000762)  
ang madagdagan po ito, at naaayon rin ho sa mandato ng batas,

**[00:52:24.320] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000763)  
especially po yung establishment ng seafarer welfare centers,

**[00:52:29.560] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000764)  
siyam po itong nakalagay sa Magna Carta, na set up na po namin sa NCR,

**[00:52:35.040] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000765)  
at about to open po ang sa Iloilo.

**[00:52:37.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000766)  
Layon po namin na mabuksan din ang sa Mindanao at yung iba pa pong mga centers sa Luzon, Visayas, at sa Mindanao.

**[00:52:46.680] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000767)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:52:48.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000768)  
Can we proceed to slide 30 on reintegration?

**[00:52:58.460] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000769)  
This is our last slide, sir, for our presentation.

**[00:53:06.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000770)  
Sir, for the reintegration program, for this year, we have from January to August,

**[00:53:14.560] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000771)  
under our Balik Pinas Balik Hanap Buhay program,

**[00:53:17.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000772)  
have provided to 16,777 po na ating mga OFWs na bumalik,

**[00:53:25.620] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000773)  
including po yung mga miyembro po ng OWA na galing po sa krisis sa gitnang silangan,

**[00:53:33.440] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000774)  
ay binigyan po natin ng pansimula para sa mga iba't iba po nilang mga livelihood na mga initiatives.

**[00:53:41.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000775)  
Of course, bago natin po sila bigyan ng ganito,

**[00:53:45.760] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000776)  
nagkakaroon po ng entrepreneurship initiatives,

**[00:53:47.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000777)  
development training para sa kanila.

**[00:53:50.740] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000778)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:53:53.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000779)  
Tatlo po, Mr. Chair, out of four,

**[00:53:56.280] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000780)  
ng livelihood beneficiaries are women.

**[00:53:58.880] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000781)  
78% po sila.

**[00:54:01.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000782)  
Sa BPBH alone, sir, medyo mas mataas,

**[00:54:04.840] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000783)  
at 80% po ay kababaihan po ang tumatanggap ng tulong mula sa OWA patungkol sa reintegration.

**[00:54:13.100] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000784)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:54:16.520] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000785)  
Kasama at matatanggap,

**[00:54:17.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000786)  
sa NRN or National Reintegration Network na ang aming pong Mother Department through the NRCO ay nagkumulma ng reintegration framework, ang Kalinga, Kaalaman at Kabuhayan.

**[00:54:36.020] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000787)  
At ang tugon ng OWA bilang parte ng NRN framework na ito ay i-open up ang aming iba't ibang mga pasilidad para magkaroon ng oportunidad ng upskilling at training at hindi lamang yan maitawid yun sa paghanap ng trabaho o pagsimula po ng negosyo.

**[00:55:01.740] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000788)  
Ngayon pong October 8 ay layo namin sanang simulan ito.

**[00:55:06.020] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000789)  
Through the step-up program, again maraming salamat po sa ating sa Senado sa bigiligay na suporta po. Ito po ay kasama dun sa 500 milyong na ipigay sa amin.

**[00:55:17.340] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000790)  
Panghuli na po dahil ito po Mr. Chair ay kayo po ang nagpondo nito nung umpisa. Ito po ang Workers Auxiliary Support Project.

**[00:55:27.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000791)  
Ito po ang katuwang ng Action Fund na pumupondo sa mga paralegal at mga legal retainers po ng OWA.

**[00:55:35.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000792)  
Tumutulong po.

**[00:55:36.020] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000793)  
At sa suporta po ng aming mahal na kalihim, gusto po namin madagdagan yung tinatawag namang medical retainers. Marami po tayo mga kababayan abroad na hindi na po nako-cover ng insurance o naging pasunang insurance, kailangan po nila ng medical support, libreng konsultasyon, libreng gamot, libreng laboratorio kahit po abroad.

**[00:56:02.400] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000794)  
Yan po ang na-expertise.

**[00:56:06.020] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000795)  
Yan po namin masuportahan sa dagdag na 130 milyon naman po dito po sa Workers Auxiliary Support Project.

**[00:56:14.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000796)  
Mr. Chair, dito na lang po at tatapusin ko na po ang presentation ng OWA. Mamaya na lang po yung ibang detalye sa mga tanong po ninyo. Maraming salamat po.

**[01:08:56.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001124)

**[01:08:58.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001125)  
And, yun nga po,

**[01:09:00.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001126)

**[01:09:02.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001127)  
na binibigay doon sa mga hindi na uwi.

**[01:09:05.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001128)  
Meron hong hindi na uwi,

**[01:09:07.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001129)  
pero dahil naapektuhan

**[01:09:08.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001130)

**[01:09:11.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001131)

**[01:09:13.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001132)  
work shifts, nabawasan ng OT,

**[01:09:15.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001133)

**[01:09:16.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001134)

**[01:09:19.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001135)  
ang 20,000 na katao

**[01:09:20.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001136)

**[01:09:22.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001137)

**[01:09:25.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001138)

**[01:09:27.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001139)

**[01:09:28.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001140)  
mga apektadong bansa, binigyan po

**[01:09:30.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001141)

**[01:09:33.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001142)  
dollars each. And also, on top of

**[01:09:35.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001143)  
that, yung grocery, basic

**[01:09:36.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001144)  
items po na binigay din natin, food,

**[01:09:38.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001145)  
water, and other basic necessities.

**[01:09:41.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001146)  
Sir, last year

**[01:09:43.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001147)  
marami po tayong kababayan ng mga pinauwi

**[01:09:44.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001148)  
na nasa cruise ship

**[01:09:46.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001149)  
galing po ng Amerika. Kumusta

**[01:09:48.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001150)  
na po sila? Nabigyan po ba ng trabaho?

**[01:09:51.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001151)  
Nakabalik na ba ang iba sa kanila

**[01:09:52.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001152)  
sa cruise ship ulit?

**[01:09:55.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001153)  
Yung mga galing po ng Caribbean?

**[01:09:56.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001154)  
Ayon sa aming talaan,

**[01:09:58.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001155)  
ay higit kumulang ay

**[01:10:00.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001156)  
222 po sila

**[01:10:03.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001157)  
between

**[01:10:04.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001158)  
2025 and 2026.

**[01:10:07.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001159)  
Nabigyan po ng

**[01:10:08.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001160)  
financial assistance at patuloy po tayong

**[01:10:11.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001161)  
namamahagi ng financial assistance

**[01:10:12.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001162)  
sa kanila. Meron

**[01:10:14.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001163)  
pong iba na nabigyan ng

**[01:10:16.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001164)  
tulong in terms of

**[01:10:18.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001165)  
pagkahanap ng trabaho. But admittedly,

**[01:10:21.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001166)  
Mr. Senator,

**[01:10:23.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001167)  
hirap po silang makahanap

**[01:10:25.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001168)  
ng trabaho sa

**[01:10:26.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001169)  
cruise ship.

**[01:10:28.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001170)  
Lalo na dun sa

**[01:10:30.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001171)  
same area, sa Americas.

**[01:10:33.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001172)  
But we are still trying our best

**[01:10:34.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001173)  
to locate fine jobs for them

**[01:10:36.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001174)  
at this stage. Kahit

**[01:10:38.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001175)  
locally po o kahit sa hotel

**[01:10:40.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001176)  
hospitality sector. Halimbawa, tutal

**[01:10:42.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001177)  
nasa hospitality sector naman ang

**[01:10:44.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001178)  
cruise industry.

**[01:10:46.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001179)  
Pang huling katanungan na lang,

**[01:10:48.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001180)  
Mr. Chair, if you would allow me.

**[01:10:51.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001181)  
Secretary, bakit po ganun?

**[01:10:53.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001182)  
Marami po tayong

**[01:10:54.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001183)  
mga embassies

**[01:10:55.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001184)  
or may marami tayong mga bansa na marami po

**[01:10:58.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001185)  
ika nga OFWs. Pero wala tayong

**[01:11:00.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001186)  
DMW office. Meron tayong

**[01:11:02.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001187)  
migrant officers sa mga embahada

**[01:11:05.120] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001188)  
naka-station. Bakit po

**[01:11:07.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001189)  
ganun? Parang marami-rami

**[01:11:09.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001190)  
pa rin tayong mga lugar

**[01:11:10.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001191)  
na wala pong

**[01:11:11.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001192)  
my office of the DMW.

**[01:11:15.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001193)  
May mga officers

**[01:11:17.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001194)  
lang po na naka-assign sa embahada

**[01:11:19.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001195)  
na yun.

**[01:11:21.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001196)  
43 po ang ating

**[01:11:23.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001197)  
migrant workers offices and they are

**[01:11:25.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001198)  
all located in areas where

**[01:11:27.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001199)  
OFWs are highly concentrated.

**[01:11:29.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001200)  
So halimbawa po sa Middle East

**[01:11:31.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001201)  
kung saan 60 to 70 percent

**[01:11:33.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001202)  
of our OFWs are located.

**[01:11:35.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001203)  
Lahat po ng bansa sa Middle East

**[01:11:37.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001204)  
ay may presensya po tayo.

**[01:11:39.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001205)  
However, admittedly, Mr. Senator,

**[01:11:41.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001206)  
ang DFA, tinatawag na

**[01:11:42.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001207)  
Foreign Service Posts, are present in around

**[01:11:45.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001208)  
95 countries. And according

**[01:11:47.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001209)  
to the DMW Charter, may bubunuin

**[01:11:49.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001210)  
po tayo na

**[01:11:50.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001211)  
kukumpletuhin natin yun.

**[01:11:53.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001212)  
Mula po nung itinalaga ang DMW,

**[01:11:55.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001213)  
meron po tayong apat

**[01:11:57.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001214)  
naging additional

**[01:11:57.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001215)  
at meron po tayong napipintong

**[01:12:00.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001216)  
apat pa, hopefully

**[01:12:03.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001217)  
before by the first

**[01:12:04.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001218)  
quarter of next year. So halimbawa

**[01:12:07.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001219)  
po, merong kanina dumaan lang

**[01:12:08.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001220)  
sa amin yung bagong labor

**[01:12:10.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001221)  
attache natin for Guam. Kasi yun

**[01:12:12.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001222)  
po ang latest natin. Ang 44th post

**[01:12:14.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001223)  
po natin ngayon is Guam.

**[01:12:17.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001224)  
Kung saan, higit kumulang

**[01:12:19.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001225)  
may mga 6 to 7 thousand

**[01:12:20.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001226)  
Filipino workers po tayo.

**[01:12:22.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001227)  
So in the past, due to

**[01:12:24.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001228)  
personnel and budgetary constraints,

**[01:12:27.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001229)  
talagang nag-focus dun sa kusaan pinakamarami.

**[01:12:30.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001230)  
At any rate,

**[01:12:31.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001231)  
Mr. Senator, 90% of

**[01:12:33.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001232)  
where our Filipinos are, may

**[01:12:35.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001233)  
labat, may migrant workers office.

**[01:12:37.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001234)  
Dun lang po tayo sa around 10%

**[01:12:39.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001235)  
na balanse na

**[01:12:41.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001236)  
meron pa rin yung OFWs

**[01:12:43.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001237)  
pero mas county po ang bilang nila

**[01:12:45.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001238)  
and in the countries

**[01:12:47.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001239)  
na hindi

**[01:12:49.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001240)  
ganung kalaki ang demand

**[01:12:51.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001241)  
sa kanila, so to speak. But nevertheless,

**[01:12:53.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001242)  
under the law, we are required to cover

**[01:12:55.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001243)  
all 95 foreign

**[01:12:56.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001244)  
service posts ng DFA.

**[01:12:59.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001245)  
Mr. Chair, I'm going

**[01:13:00.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001246)  
to attend the dep-dep.

**[01:13:02.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001247)  
I just want to

**[01:13:03.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001248)  
inform the body, Mr. Chair, that

**[01:13:06.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001249)  
I support the

**[01:13:08.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001250)  
budget proposal of

**[01:13:11.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001251)  
the DMW

**[01:13:13.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001252)  
and OWA

**[01:13:14.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001253)  
and probably even add more,

**[01:13:17.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001254)  
Mr. Chair, on the Emergency Repatriation

**[01:13:19.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001255)  
Fund and then the

**[01:13:21.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001256)  
Action Fund of

**[01:13:22.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001257)  
OWA or of DMW.

**[01:13:25.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001258)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[01:13:26.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001259)  
Thank you. Thank you very much, Senator Erwin.

**[01:13:28.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001260)  
Sir, can I just provide an update dun sa

**[01:13:30.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001261)  
dinalaw niyo po ay siya po

**[01:13:33.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001262)  
ay still remains in the same facility

**[01:13:35.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001263)  
where you found her.

**[01:13:37.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001264)  
Hospice care na po kasi.

**[01:13:39.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001265)  
Kaya po hindi siya

**[01:13:41.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001266)  
mailabas at this

**[01:13:43.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001267)  
stage. Hospice care na po siya. But she's

**[01:13:45.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001268)  
still there and she's being taken care of

**[01:13:47.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001269)  
and monitored by DMW OWA.

**[01:13:49.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001270)  
Thank you. Thank you, Secretary.

**[01:13:51.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001271)  
Now we give the floor, of course, to

**[01:13:52.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001272)  
the idol here in the

**[01:13:55.080] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001273)  
Senate, our idol,

**[01:13:56.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001274)  
Senator Rafi Tulfo, who is

**[01:13:58.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001275)  
also very supportive and very

**[01:14:00.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001276)  
interested in ensuring the safety

**[01:14:02.940] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001277)  
of our migrant workers. Sir, you have

**[01:14:04.840] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001278)  
the floor, sir.

**[01:14:05.610] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001278-split-2)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:14:07.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001279)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair,

**[01:14:09.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001280)  
Senator Joel Villanueva and our resource

**[01:14:10.860] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001281)  
persons from the Department of Migrant Workers

**[01:14:13.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001282)  
and OWA, led by

**[01:14:15.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001283)  
Secretary Hans Kakdak and

**[01:14:16.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001284)  
Administrator P.Y. Kaunan.

**[01:14:19.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001285)  
I'm here today to express

**[01:14:21.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001286)  
my full and firm support

**[01:14:23.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001287)  
for the budgets of the Department of

**[01:14:25.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001288)  
Migrant Workers and OWA.

**[01:14:26.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001289)  
Which provide the resources needed

**[01:14:29.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001290)  
to protect and uphold the rights

**[01:14:31.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001291)  
of nearly 2.1

**[01:14:33.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001292)  
million OFWs

**[01:14:35.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001293)  
worldwide. Alam na po natin

**[01:14:37.400] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001294)  
lahat ng ating mga OFWs

**[01:14:38.960] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001295)  
ang sandigan ng ating ekonomiya.

**[01:14:41.480] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001296)  
Lalo na sa pamagitan ng kanilang

**[01:14:43.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001297)  
remittances. Kaya

**[01:14:45.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001298)  
malagang tiyakin ang proteksyon

**[01:14:47.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001299)  
ng kanilang karapatan at kapakanan.

**[01:14:50.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001300)  
At kasabay nito,

**[01:14:51.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001301)  
siguruhing may sapat,

**[01:14:53.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001302)  
maagap, at tuloy-tuloy na

**[01:14:54.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001303)  
pondo ang DMW.

**[01:14:56.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001304)  
Upang may atid ang mga servisyong

**[01:14:59.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001305)  
na rapat para sa...

**[01:15:00.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001306)  
sa ating mga makabagong bayani.

**[01:15:02.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001307)  
As Chairman of the Senate Committee on Migrant Workers,

**[01:15:05.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001308)  
may apat na key areas na sana'y mapunduhan natin ng sapat

**[01:15:09.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001309)  
para masigurong mabuti at nigtas ang kalagayan ng lahat ng ating mga OFWs.

**[01:15:15.480] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001310)  
Number one, swift crisis response or immediate assistance for distressed OFWs caught in crisis

**[01:15:23.560] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001311)  
through the what we call Action Fund.

**[01:15:26.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001312)  
Number two, safe repatriation and reintegration for returning OFWs,

**[01:15:33.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001313)  
especially those who come home because of abuse, calamities, illegal recruitment or human trafficking.

**[01:15:40.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001314)  
Number three, healthcare and education support not only for OFWs but also for their families here in the Philippines.

**[01:15:48.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001315)  
And finally, number four, proper monitoring to ensure that foreign employers strictly comply

**[01:15:55.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001316)  
with labor laws.

**[01:15:56.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001317)  
Including establishment of accessible hotlines and digital platforms

**[01:16:01.560] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001318)  
where OFWs can immediately seek help in cases of abuse.

**[01:16:06.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001319)  
Mr. Chair, we must remember that every peso allocated to and spent by the DMW and OWA

**[01:16:12.200] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001320)  
is an investment in the safety, dignity and future of our OFWs.

**[01:16:18.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001321)  
With that, I have some questions.

**[01:16:21.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001322)  
Yes, Senator Rafi.

**[01:16:24.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001323)  
We fully take note of your...

**[01:16:26.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001324)  
Four points.

**[01:16:27.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001325)  
Swift crisis response, safe repatriation and reintegration, support to OFWs and their families.

**[01:16:36.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001326)  
And I think one of the most important points you mentioned is the proper monitoring of our OFWs while they are working abroad.

**[01:16:45.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001327)  
You may proceed with your questions, Senator Rafi.

**[01:16:48.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001328)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:16:49.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001329)

**[01:16:53.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001330)

**[01:16:55.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001331)  
Anong nangyari?

**[01:16:56.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001332)

**[01:16:57.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001333)

**[01:17:00.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001334)  
Siguro walang kamaganak ng OFW to.

**[01:17:02.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001335)

**[01:17:05.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001336)

**[01:17:12.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001337)

**[01:17:18.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001338)

**[01:17:22.560] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001339)  
Ano pong binigay na reason?

**[01:17:23.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001340)

**[01:17:26.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001341)  
Ah, Mr. Chair, ang sa pagkakaalam ko po, ah, mawalang gahalang sa DBM,

**[01:17:32.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001342)

**[01:17:37.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001343)  
kung saan medyo mababata-bata pa sa edad ang departamento namin.

**[01:17:41.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001344)

**[01:17:45.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001345)  
Pero definitely, Mr. Chair, ah, we hit our stride in 2025.

**[01:17:49.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001346)

**[01:17:56.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001347)

**[01:17:57.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001348)

**[01:17:59.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001349)  
Ah, kami ni Admin PY, ah, ubos ang aming pondo, ah, dahil sa U.S.-Israel-Iran War.

**[01:18:06.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001350)  
Ah, but mukhang lagpas na po yata sa utilization review.

**[01:18:12.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001351)  
Itong mga period na binabanggit ko.

**[01:18:15.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001352)  
At, ah, the DBM, ah, of course, ah, they are our partners also, ah, Mr. Chair, Secretary Kim.

**[01:18:21.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001353)

**[01:18:22.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001354)  
Opo.

**[01:18:22.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001355)  
Sek.

**[01:18:22.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001356)  
Ah, nag-appeal din po, Mr. Senator.

**[01:18:26.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001357)

**[01:18:39.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001360)

**[01:18:44.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001361)

po between Admin PY and ourselves, ah, 2 billion pong hinihingi namin, bali 4 billion pong karagdagan.

**[01:18:56.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001363)  
Mr. Chair, ah, kasi, almost every day, sa program ako, sumanda sila sa Action Center,

**[01:19:02.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001364)

**[01:19:06.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001365)

**[01:19:09.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001366)  
Ah, hindi maganda yung kalagayan at pagtrato at nakakaawa talaga.

**[01:19:14.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001367)  
Eh, nakikita ko naman yung DMW natin at OFW, ayaw yung, yung OWA, eh, nagtatrabaho naman talaga

**[01:19:19.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001368)  
to ensure na sila'y mapabalik dito.

**[01:19:21.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001369)  
At, ah, malaking pakinabang talaga ng mga OFW sa dalawang nabanggit.

**[01:19:26.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001370)  
Ito yung mga agency or department. The problem lang here is kulang sa budget ngayon sa nakikita ko. Paano silang makagalaw kung tali naman yung kanilang mga kamay when it comes to budget dahil imbis na i-consider ng DPM at pag-aralan yung kanilang proposed budget, pinutulan pa.

**[01:19:50.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001371)  
Now, Mr. Chair, dito sa 2 billion na nirequest nila, 1 billion sa Action Fund, sa DMW ito, 1 billion sa MOOE, and then sa OWWA, 2 billion, 500 for Emergency Repatriation Fund. Hindi pa kayong pagbigyan ito?

**[01:20:08.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001372)  
Mr. Senator, if I will be asked about it, I am fully supporting it and with your help, we will fight for it in the budget deliberation.

**[01:20:19.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001373)  
So how do we go about na i-push natin ito? Ito sa mga tinatawag nating makabagong bayani at alam naman natin kahit na noong pampanahon ay nagkagewang-gewang na ekonomiya ng mundo, nagkaroon ng world crisis, lumulutang-lutang pa rin tayo dahil nandiyan po yung ating mga OFWs na nakapack up, nakasuporta sa atin through their remittances.

**[01:20:40.400] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001374)  
Ngayon, kailangan nila natin tulong through the DMW and OWA. Siguro naman, we really need to support.

**[01:20:49.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001375)  
The DMW and OWA na sila nagkaalaga sa ating mga OFWs. So how do we go about pagdating sa budget deliberation? May ibigay itong 2 billion?

**[01:20:58.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001376)  
Yes, tama po kayo. Record high yung pinapadala ng OFWs na 39.62 billion US dollars in personal remittances.

**[01:21:08.940] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001377)  
Ang sa atin lang po ay alam nyo naman how budgeting works when you increase the budget of a particular agency or increase.

**[01:21:19.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001378)  
The budget of an item here, we have to find out saan natin na po pwedeng kuhanin.

**[01:21:27.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001379)  
Isa po kanina dun sa napag-usapan nga natin, isa sa nakita natin kanina, Senator Rafi, merong nilagyan ng pondo yung personal services pero wala namang item.

**[01:21:38.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001380)  
So mukhang hindi nila mapapakinabangan yung dinagdag na pondo dun sa OWA dahil wala namang item, wala namang plantilya.

**[01:21:47.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001381)  
So that's 200 million already, but according to our friends from OWA, kung malabas naman yung plantilya, kaya nilang punan ito.

**[01:21:57.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001382)  
So we'll find ways and of course with your help and the support of Senator Javis, our finance chair, he gave me assurance that he will join us later on today as we look into the budget of DMW and OWA.

**[01:22:14.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001383)  
Okay, thank you. Dalawang tanong na lamang.

**[01:22:17.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001384)  
Mr. Chair, Sec. Hans, doon po sa mga seafarers natin na ayaw maglayag dahil doon sa mga drone strikes, natakot sila, ayaw nilang pumalaot, ito mga tripulanting ito, anong tulong ang pwedeng maibigay sa mga ito?

**[01:22:39.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001385)  
Yes po, ang bilang po natin, Mr. Senator, Mr. Chair, is 1,500 po.

**[01:22:46.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001388)  
In the last three years, na itinalaga natin yung issuance natin, pagkilala ng right to refuse sailing, 1,500 po ang nag-manifest.

**[01:22:55.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001389)  
However, Mr. Senator, nakalagay po doon sa 1,500, nakalagay po sa rules natin on right to refuse sailing, they should not be discriminated against.

**[01:23:06.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001390)  
At ang magandang balita po dito, the 1,500 who have been reported to us and affirmed by their respective license mining agencies, hindi naman po silang pinagbabawalan.

**[01:23:16.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001391)  
So, as far as we know, wala pong naapekto hang nag-exercise ng right to refuse sailing dahil marami naman pong rota ang mga shipping lines at they are just merely transferred from one voyage, one assignment to another.

**[01:23:35.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001392)  
But having said that, yun pong mga umuuwi ay binibigyan po natin ng action fund benefit as they come home, Mr. Senator.

**[01:23:46.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001393)  
Second, saan pa mas mga kamura tayo yung marami tayong tie-up sa mga different hotels partners natin kapag may mga dumarating ng mga OFWs before they are sent back to their homes in the provinces?

**[01:24:00.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001394)  
So, chine-check-in sila sa tulad ng kabayano, hotel and all other hotels versus kung tayo ay magtayo ng mga accommodation houses.

**[01:24:11.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001395)  
Kasi paano kung puno yung hotel at that time dumating ng mga OFWs, marami sila, kunyari 20 eh.

**[01:24:16.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001396)  
Wala na palang room na available.

**[01:24:18.780] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001397)  
Kung tayo ay magtatayo ng sarili natin mga accommodation houses para sa mga repatriated OFWs papunta probinsya, which one sa tingin ninyo mas more practical?

**[01:24:29.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001398)  
Yung mag-tie-up sa mga hotel o magpatayo tayo ng mga accommodation houses?

**[01:24:32.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001399)  
Ako pong tingin ko po dyan at ito lumabas na yung issue neto ng COVID.

**[01:24:37.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001400)  
Kung ang scale po ng repatriation is a global pandemic o a global conflict, global crisis,

**[01:24:44.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001401)  
kung saan libo o daang libo

**[01:24:46.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001402)  
ang umuuwi, mainam po na yung private sector po ang mag-handle.

**[01:24:50.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001403)  
Yung hospitality sector who has greater expertise sa pangangalaga ng tao.

**[01:24:55.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001404)  
Katulad nung nangyari po sa COVID.

**[01:24:57.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001405)  
At hindi po kaya ng gobyerno na magtayo ng accommodation with let's say 1,000, 2,000, 10,000 rooms.

**[01:25:06.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001406)  
Wala po yan sa linya ng trabaho ng gobyerno.

**[01:25:09.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001407)  
I think in the past, sinubukan na po yan, government-run hotels.

**[01:25:12.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001408)

**[01:25:16.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001409)  
However, Mr. Senator, Mr. Chair, ay pwede naman po sa tingin ko, kasi ginawa na rin po ng OWA ito,

**[01:25:24.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001410)  
pwede naman po magpatakbo itong plano na rin ni Admin PY ito, ng smaller scale.

**[01:25:30.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001411)  
So halimbawa, kung 50 rooms, 25 to 50 rooms, pansamantalang paglalagyan, pwede naman po yun.

**[01:25:38.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001412)  
Pero on a bigger scale, sa tingin ko po, hindi kaya ng pamahalaan ito.

**[01:25:43.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001413)

**[01:25:44.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001414)  
At ang katapusan po niyan, Mr. Senator, Mr. Chair, ay magre-reklamo rin po yung mga tao na hindi nasa antas ng hospitality sector standards ang pangangalaga sa kanila.

**[01:26:01.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001415)  
So sa tingin ko po, on a smaller scale, yes, pwede po hawakan ng DMW o ng OWA.

**[01:26:08.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001416)

**[01:26:14.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001417)  
to take care of them.

**[01:26:15.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001418)  
Kabayan Hotel, most notably, has been a foremost lodging space for them.

**[01:26:22.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001419)  
Si Mr. Chair, narinig ko, nagtatanong siya, galing sa kanya itong katanungan.

**[01:26:26.640] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001420)  
Ano na rin?

**[01:26:29.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001421)  
Ano na rin nangyari dun sa kaso ng...

**[01:26:32.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001422)  
Sa halfway house ni Mr. Ignacio.

**[01:26:37.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001423)

**[01:26:41.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001424)  
Una, recommending the filing of...

**[01:26:44.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001425)

**[01:26:50.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001426)

**[01:26:57.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001427)

**[01:27:08.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001428)

**[01:27:10.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001429)

**[01:27:14.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001430)

**[01:27:38.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001437)

**[01:27:43.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001438)  
So, lumalabas.

**[01:27:44.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001439)

**[01:27:48.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001440)  
Mukhang hindi po na-pass upan yun ng Ubudzman sa resolution.

**[01:27:52.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001441)  
Baka nakaligtaan lang.

**[01:27:54.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001442)

**[01:27:57.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001443)  
Kasi halagang 97 million pesos po yun.

**[01:27:59.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001444)

**[01:28:03.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001445)

Thank you.

**[01:37:57.620] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001658)  
Mr. Chair, with the permission of the Secretary, as regards to utilization of OWAS, may I first focus on the Emergency Repatriation Fund. Pangalan pa lang po, by nature, kung ako lang po, dapat ko sana.

**[01:38:13.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001659)  
In a perfect world, Mr. Chair, walang magasos noon. Ibig sabihin, walang naging problema, walang naging emergency. Every year po, iba ang itsura or landscape ng mga iba't-ibang emergencies that we're facing.

**[01:38:29.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001660)  
So, for last year, iba po ang last year sa this year at iba ang this year sa next year. But ang pwede po natin gawin, and as we have always done, is to be ready.

**[01:38:41.820] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001661)

**[01:39:03.400] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001662)  
That's why we ask for supplemental for this year. On supplemental, Mr. Chair, 18% na po ng supplemental, which is on the part of the ERF, 700 million, 18% po doon obligated na. **[01:39:31.520] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001663)  
As to absorptive capacity of the agency of OWA, Mr. Chair, as of August 31, 2026, hindi lang po ang ERF kundi yung buong current appropriation ng OWA, ang utilization rate na po natin ay 72%. In other words, Mr. Chair, more than on track na po tayo para sa isang magandang utilization rate for the year.

**[01:39:58.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001664)  
But as I said, pag sinasabi natin, matatagal.

**[01:40:01.520] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001665)  
Masang utilization, Mr. Chair, ibig sabihin marami yung emergencies at maraming problema ang sinasagot at sinusolusyonan. So yun po ang special nature siguro, Mr. Chair, ng both action fund and emergency repatriation. It is really the cost of being ready.

**[01:40:19.980] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001666)  
And if I may add, Mr. Chair, this is on record. We have requested, we requested for supplemental budget for the emergency repatriation fund.

**[01:40:31.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001667)  
And I believe the action fund. As early as April of this year, we even, I think, sent a correspondence to the DBM as early as I think late March. March, which is three months lang pong nakalipas dun sa taong ito.

**[01:40:49.520] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001668)  
We already requested and we have received on the part of OWA the supplemental budget, I think by end of July or first week of August.

**[01:41:01.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001669)  
So, importante po sa amin na kung tatanungin niyo po ako, Mr. Chair, at the rate of utilization or obligation of OWA right now of 206 million of the ERF every year, every month.

**[01:41:18.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001670)  
Next year po, kung 900 plus million, mga ano lang po yan, mga 4.7 months.

**[01:41:29.820] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001671)  
At yung dinadagdag po namin 500 million, mga kulang-kulang 3 million. So, mga walong buwan po yan kung titignan yung utilization rate ngayon.

**[01:41:40.200] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001672)

I still remember those days na I think we were able to tackle the bill on establishing the Department of Migrant Workers for almost two years, if I'm not mistaken.

**[02:29:13.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002339)  
And every time magkakaroon.

**[02:29:15.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002340)  
And every time magkakaroon ng mga roadblocks, pitstops, etc., si Sen. Bong Go yung pinaka talagang most dependable individual na kausap ko noon para i-push yung bill.

**[02:29:28.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002341)  
And true enough, naging batas po ito.

**[02:29:31.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002342)  
So we really put it into the records of this hall na malaking tulong yung ginawa ni Sen. Bong Go to establish the Department of Migrant Workers.

**[02:29:42.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002343)  
So again, thank you, Sen. Bong Go.

**[02:29:45.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002344)  
Especially now na nandyan na, hindi mo pa rin iniiwan at sumusuport pa rin sa budgetary requirements and requests ng DMW.

**[02:29:54.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002345)  
Thank you, Sen. Bong Go.

**[02:29:55.320] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002346)  
May I add something?

**[02:30:00.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002347)  
Alam niyo, nung panahon si Senator Joel, siya yung chairperson ng Labor Committee dito sa Senado. Muntik na siyang nawala ng pag-asa pero hindi po siya bumigay. Talagang pinaglaban niya. Sabi ko, boss, alang-alang na lang sa OFWs na, mga kababayan nating OFWs, please lang, huwag kang bumigay, paglaban mo pa rin, huwag kang mawala ng pag-asa.

**[02:30:24.540] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002348)  
At true enough, yung ating panaginip nagkatotoo po na nagkaroon ng Department of Migrant Workers. Salamat sa iyong pagmamahal sa ating mga OFWs, Senator Joel Villanueva. Maraming salamat po.

**[02:30:37.060] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002349)  
Thank you again, Senator Bong. And let me stem my next questions dun sa binabanggit ka rin ni Senator Bong. Una yung sa Passport Act. I'm one of the authors of the 10 years validity of passports.

**[02:30:54.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002350)  
And yung APO publishing is under OP now. Is that correct? It's under OP now. Before, it's under BSP. So right now, if I'm not mistaken, it's under OP.

**[02:31:07.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002351)  
And I heard Secretary Tess Lazaro saying that she was able to talk to APO and nag-request na po sila. It's just that siguro concerted efforts, lahat tayo, magpagtalong-talungan po natin.

**[02:31:24.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002352)  
Hindi maganda, especially right now, na itaas yung... And I'm with Senator Bong on this particular issue. Now, let me continue my questions with the Action Fund. Kasi tinitignan ko rin. Yung sa DFA, binanggit ko, yung legal fund nila is 200 million. Still there. Meron pa silang 1 billion na assistance to national. I'm not trying to get funding from DFA.

**[02:31:51.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002353)  
But in all honesty, when I...

**[02:31:54.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002354)  
I asked that question as to the utilization ng department, I think mataas pa rin yung available funds nila dito. I just don't know kung ngayong time of the year ba talagang ganon. I missed the budget briefing last time.

**[02:32:15.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002355)  
But if you look at the Action Fund, Secretary and the Admin Kaunan, pareho yung...

**[02:32:24.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002356)  
Yung Action Fund, merong repatriation, meron din ang OWA. Pati yung Action Fund, ang dami. May legal, may medical, repatriation. Merong...

**[02:32:35.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002357)  
Yun nga kanina binabanggit ni Sec Hans, yung mga distressed OFWs, yung may kailangan ng tulong because they were victimized by illegal recruitment, etc.

**[02:32:50.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002358)  
And then you have...

**[02:32:54.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002359)  
Reintegration, nandyan din po, nakaladge.

**[02:32:57.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002360)  
Now, looking at dun sa repatriation, hindi ko ba pag may nirepatriate tayo, of course, inuuna natin kaagad dyan dahil emergency yan eh. Most urgent yan na kailangang tulungan.

**[02:33:13.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002361)  
But the companies, the employers, they are also somehow liable in...

**[02:33:21.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002362)  
In...

**[02:33:22.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002363)  
For instance...

**[02:33:24.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002364)  
Giving help dun sa medical assistance, dun po sa pagbili, for example, ng tickets, plane tickets, etc.

**[02:33:34.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002365)  
May po ba tayong kaukulang pondo na nakukuha pa from their end, yung reimbursement na nakukuha?

**[02:33:44.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002366)  
And at the same time, gaano po ang percentage-wise na nakaka-comply dito ng mga kumpanya?

**[02:33:53.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002367)  
Or...

**[02:33:54.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002368)  
Or employers na nakukuha pa po natin?

**[02:33:58.740] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002369)  
Secretary?

**[02:34:00.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002370)  
Tama po kayo, Mr. Senator.

**[02:34:03.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002371)  
The main responsibility to repatriate is with the employer and the mining agency.

**[02:34:11.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002372)  
For those who are still covered by their first two years contracts, kusa nandun pa yung agency.

**[02:34:18.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002373)  
But we're fully aware that most of OFWs, around 70% of OFWs,

**[02:34:24.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002374)  
are tinatawag na balik manggagawa.

**[02:34:27.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002375)  
Ibig sabihin, more than two years na sila sa abroad, at wala na silang agency covered under the compulsory insurance laws,

**[02:34:35.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002376)  
where repatriation will be covered by insurance company policy provided by the insurance company to the agency as the policyholder.

**[02:34:47.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002377)  
Ngayon, ang OWA po ang may mechanism doon ng reimbursement mechanism.

**[02:34:52.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002378)  
So, si...

**[02:34:54.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002379)  
Admin PY, baka may kaukulang ulat po sa refunding.

**[02:34:57.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002380)  
Pero, in general, pag-crisis ang sitwasyon, we don't pause to say,

**[02:35:04.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002381)  
hindi kami yan, agency yan, etc.

**[02:35:07.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002382)  
So, tama po kayo, refund ang mekanismo natin.

**[02:35:11.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002383)  
Ngayon, doon po sa medical, tama po kayo, many OFWs,

**[02:35:16.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002384)  
whether balik manggagawa or baguhan na OFWs, may insurance naman po sa host country, in general.

**[02:35:23.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002385)  
So, sa kanina,

**[02:35:24.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002386)  
binanggit nyo, just for me, my personal education, two years lang po yung compulsory.

**[02:35:29.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002387)  
For repatriation, yes.

**[02:35:30.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002388)  
For repatriation, two years.

**[02:35:31.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002389)  
Yes, two years.

**[02:35:32.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002390)  
Pero sa medical, whether balik manggagawa...

**[02:35:34.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002391)  
Sa medical, as long as they're covered by the medical insurance laws, regulations in the host country,

**[02:35:39.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002392)  
and in general, they are all, they are covered.

**[02:35:42.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002393)  
So, inuuna po natin munang i-avail nila.

**[02:35:45.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002394)  
Habang may na-hospital ng OFW, hindi po natin agad sinasagot sa abroad yung hospital bills.

**[02:35:50.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002395)  
Dahil malamang sa malamang, covered siya ng insurance doon.

**[02:35:53.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002396)  
So,

**[02:35:54.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002397)  
So, tine-exhaust po muna yung insurance policy coverage.

**[02:35:59.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002398)  
Pag nagkulang na po, o di kaya undocumented, kung saan walang insurance, doon po tayo pumapasok.

**[02:36:05.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002399)  
So, rest assured, hindi po tayo pumapasok agad kung covered naman ng insurance yung kababayan natin, hinahayaan muna natin.

**[02:36:15.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002400)  
Doon po sa matter on the other matters like refund, baka si admin PY pumakapasok.

**[02:36:21.400] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002401)  
Yes, admin PY.

**[02:36:22.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002402)  
And I ask this question because I wanted to make sure...

**[02:36:24.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002403)  
I'm sure na yung mining agencies are also doing their share, doing their part.

**[02:36:29.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002404)  
And I wanted to understand where it goes, yung reimbursement, etc.

**[02:36:34.400] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002405)  
Saan napupunta? Inaano ba natin yun?

**[02:36:38.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002406)  
Dinapalik sa treasury or pwede rin natin gamitin na pondo para ipanggamit ng OWA?

**[02:36:47.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002407)  
Ma'am?

**[02:36:48.500] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002408)  
Thank you very much, Chair, doon po sa tanong na yan.

**[02:36:53.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002409)  
Una-una po, simulan ko.

**[02:36:54.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002410)  
Doon sa RA 8042, Section 15, yan ang Emergency Repatriation Fund.

**[02:37:00.980] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002411)  
Nandyan mismo nakasaad na ang may primary responsibility po patungkol sa repatriation ay yung mga employers po or yung kanila pong mga recruitment agencies.

**[02:37:12.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002412)  
Pero nasabi nga po na aming kalihim, pag emergency po, alam naman kung antahin pa natin sila na umakto, kaya po meron pong way of reimbursement.

**[02:37:22.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002413)  
Okay.

**[02:37:24.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002414)  
Mr. Chair, ako na hong unang aamin na medyo mahirap kolektahin.

**[02:37:30.820] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002415)  
Kami po ay nagpapadala ng mga demand letter, nakailan na rin po kaming referral sa Department of Migrant Workers for proper administrative sanction, if that is kung hindi pa rin po sila magko-comply.

**[02:37:45.460] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002416)  
Pero over the years po, yun po ang nagiging proseso.

**[02:37:49.380] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002417)  
Merong mga, meron pong ang OWA po ang nangungunang...

**[02:37:54.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002418)  
...mag-gumastos para sa emergency repatriation, lalong-lalo na po, yung nasabi nga po ni SecHands, yung mga newly hired.

**[02:38:02.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002419)  
At pagkatapos po niyan ay we send the demand letter and then kung hindi pa rin po talaga sumagot, we refer to the, then po, POEA now, the Department of Migrant Workers.

**[02:38:17.400] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002420)  
Opo, kasi po sila po ang regulatory, sila po may regulatory function.

**[02:38:24.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002421)  
Pero po, tama po yun, malaking porsyento po kasi nang tinutulungan natin ay mga balik manggagawa na o wala na pong recruitment agency.

**[02:38:38.920] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002422)  
Mapayagan niya rin po akong sabihin and put on record, pag tinignan po namin, binalangkas po namin yung emergency repatriation fund at mas malaki po ang porsyento.

**[02:38:50.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002423)  
Actually, Mr. Chair, mga lalampas siguro ng 90%.

**[02:38:54.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002424)  
Kung titignan mo yung tulong pagtutungkol sa airfare, transportation, and shelter, ay land-based OFWs.

**[02:39:03.880] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002425)  
Ibig sabihin po, Mr. Chair, patungkol sa sea-based, ang mga manning agencies na ayon naman yan sa maritime convention at sa kanilang kontrata ay sumusunod at nagbibigay po at umaako nitong kanilang obligasyon under contract and under international maritime convention po.

**[02:39:24.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002426)  
Just to understand, Admin, pag naka-collect, what do you do with the collection?

**[02:39:33.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002427)  
Pwede bang gamitin ito ng OWA or ito'y binabalik sa treasury?

**[02:39:38.620] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002428)  
Binabalik po natin sa treasury pag emergency repatriation fund po dahil ito po ay gaa.

**[02:39:45.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002429)  
And can you give us a ballpark figure like sample last year?

**[02:39:51.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002430)  
How much are we talking about here?

**[02:39:54.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002431)  
You were able to collect, which of course you made mention, very difficult.

**[02:39:58.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002432)  
Very difficult, but for this year alone, Mr. Chair, over 10 million pa lang ang nakokolekta po namin na kumbaga naibalik sa treasury.

**[02:40:09.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002433)  
Pero yung demand letter and yun nga nasa kapending, meron kayong ballpark figure din kung magkano yung… kahit pa submit nyo na lang, just for our…

**[02:40:22.660] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002434)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[02:40:23.620] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002435)  
Just to make sure.

**[02:40:24.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002436)  
We understand.

**[02:40:26.380] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002437)  
Opo, Mr. Chair.

**[02:40:28.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002438)  
Pero hindi ko po masasabi ngayon on record at magsasumiti na lang po kami sa komite patungkol sa actual amount.

**[02:40:36.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002439)  
Pero ipauna ko na rin po, Mr. Chair, ang hinahabolan po namin ay 176 Philippine recruitment agencies.

**[02:40:45.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002440)  
Ang bayad pa lang po ay 13.

**[02:40:47.760] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002441)  
So yun po ay we will submit to the committee a more comprehensive report on this, Mr. Chair.

**[02:40:53.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002442)  
Thank you.

**[02:40:54.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002443)  
Appreciate it.

**[02:40:54.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002444)  
Let me go to another point dito sa part ng ating emergency protection ng ating mga kababayan.

**[02:41:08.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002445)  
May we know right now how many OFWs remain in conflict affected or yung nasa kategory ng high risk areas?

**[02:41:18.940] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002446)  
And of course, what level of contingency capacity

**[02:41:24.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002447)  
is required for their possible evacuation or repatriation?

**[02:41:31.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002448)  
Just to have an idea, Secretary?

**[02:41:34.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002449)  
The figure that we have po is in the two areas, mainly yung sa Gulf states, sa may Arabian Gulf, and sa tinatawag na Levant, Israel, Jordan, Lebanon.

**[02:41:47.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002450)  
What do you call that?

**[02:41:47.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002451)  
Levant?

**[02:41:48.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002452)  
Levant.

**[02:41:48.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002453)  
Levant.

**[02:41:49.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002454)  
Levant.

**[02:41:51.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002455)  
2.5 million po ang…

**[02:41:54.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002456)  
Estimate doon.

**[02:41:56.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002457)  
Opo, opo.

**[02:41:57.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002458)  
But having said that, Mr. Senator, tama po kayo.

**[02:42:01.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002459)  
This is not to cause any alarm.

**[02:42:04.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002460)  
This is not necessarily a case of 2.5 million all in danger.

**[02:42:08.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002461)  
So lalo na sa kaso ng gera na ito, talagang nakikita natin kakaiba.

**[02:42:15.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002462)  
Halimbawa, nakikita natin na targeted ang mga attacks.

**[02:42:22.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002463)  
Maaring nung simula ng gera, nakita natin.

**[02:42:24.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002464)  
May ilang civilian areas, targets na natamaan.

**[02:42:27.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002465)  
Pero in due time, nakita natin na naging targeted na mga military facilities, installations, ang ina-atake on both sides.

**[02:42:37.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002466)  
Doon po sa…

**[02:42:38.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002467)  
Ang Saudi Arabia, which is around 1 million OFWs, so at least 50% of the 2.5, nakita naman natin ay on the whole ay spared ang Saudi Arabia from the attacks.

**[02:42:50.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002468)  
Although lately, there have been some attacks also.

**[02:42:54.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002469)  
And Saudi, but in general, the biggest land mass, which is Saudi with the biggest OFW population, relatively not as intense ang attacks as compared to the other, to the smaller Gulf states.

**[02:43:08.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002470)  
So tama po kayo.

**[02:43:10.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002471)  
Kailangan inuans ito.

**[02:43:12.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002472)  
Kailangan i-contextualize.

**[02:43:13.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002473)  
Hindi pwedeng pang kalahatang sabihin na 2.5 in danger.

**[02:43:18.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002474)  
But having said that, ang napag-usapan na po natin kanina, it's still always prudent to…

**[02:43:24.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002475)  
Be ready for the worst case scenario, for a case where we will repatriate more than 12,000, which was the case last March, April, and May.

**[02:43:34.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002476)  
Thank you. Thank you for that.

**[02:43:36.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002477)  
And we trust that the Lord will intervene.

**[02:43:43.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002478)  
And we don't want this to happen again, itong nangyari, itong nakaraan.

**[02:43:49.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002479)  
And if you're talking about 2.5 million Filipinos…

**[02:43:54.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002480)  
Nandito sa areas ng Gulf State at Lebanon-Israel area.

**[02:44:00.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002481)  
Let me go to one of my most favorite topics, itong human capital development.

**[02:44:08.780] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002482)  
And when we were talking about this particular provision of the DMW's charter, we talked about full cycle reintegration sa ating mga OFWs.

**[02:44:24.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002483)  
Yung pathway linking skills assessment nila, yung training, certification, employment, and livelihood assistance.

**[02:44:38.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002484)  
How are DMW and OFW working towards this full cycle reintegration?

**[02:44:44.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002485)  
I was informed about this July 20, 2016, 2026 Joint Memorandum Circular Expansion.

**[02:44:54.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002486)  
Pwedeng yung kaalaman tungo sa Kabuhayan Initiative.

**[02:44:58.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002487)  
And I just want…

for example,

for a
